- **ai-coding-first**: 编排当前仓库的 AI Coding 文档初始化或刷新流程，并固定从当前项目根目录的 `.agents/skills/` 读取下游技能。适用于用户要求 AI Coding First、项目 coding 初始化、刷新 codebase map/coding maps/AGENTS map/harness docs、创建或优化 AGENTS.md，或希望按顺序依次调用 `$gsd-map-codebase`、`$coding-maps`、`$agents-map`、`$compound-harness-docs`、`$create-rules`，为 backend/frontend 等子项目生成或更新中文文档；如果对应文档不是中文，必须修改成中文。 ([yogogml0035-cpu/MyAgent](https://github.com/yogogml0035-cpu/MyAgent/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ai-coding-first))
- **ai-coding-fleet**: 轩辕AI编码舰队——OpenClaw作为大脑，指挥Claude Code/Cursor/Windsurf/Trae/Codex CLI集群并行开发。覆盖：ACP配置、IDE接入、权限管理、任务分发、windsurf+cursor双IDE调度、worktree隔离、舰队看板。触发词：IDE舰队、编码集群、AI IDE、多工具编排、ACP配置、Cursor舰队、Windsurf集群、Trae接入、IDE调度、并行编码、AI编码引擎、IDE编排、coding fleet ([aAAaqwq/openclaw-team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/openclaw-team/tree/HEAD/workspace/agents/workspace-xuanyuan/skills/ai-coding-fleet) · ★2)
- **ai-coding-from-china**: Set up and verify AI coding tools (Claude Code, Cursor, Cline, Codex, Cherry Studio) from mainland China — pick an access path, configure the two env vars, verify you're getting the real model (not a silent downgrade), split big/small models to cut cost, and fix the common connection errors. Use when a developer in China says "Claude Code 用不了 / 付不了", "Cursor 封 IP", "怎么在国内调 Claude/GPT API", "中转会不会降智", or is configuring an OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible relay. Vendor-neutral method; verify everything yourself. ([cocodot2026/ai-coding-from-china](https://github.com/cocodot2026/ai-coding-from-china/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **ai-coding-guardrails**: Use when an AI coding agent is writing or refactoring production code and its known failure modes need to be held in check — over-engineering, silent scope creep, invented APIs, claiming work is done without checking, deleting tests to make them pass. Trigger when the user asks for careful, production-grade, or reviewed code, or when a previous agent run produced confident output that turned out wrong. ([OneWave-AI/claude-skills](https://github.com/OneWave-AI/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/ai-coding-guardrails) · ★435 · 2 copies)
- **ai-coding-guardrails**: 在已有项目维护、缺陷修复、增量修改，或用户强调避免“改好一处又改坏另一处”时使用。指导分析影响范围、控制改动、验证关联功能并如实汇报。新模块接入旧系统时仅约束涉及既有代码、数据和接口的部分；不把从零开发套入维护流程。 ([1058772968-dotcom/ai-coding-guardrails](https://github.com/1058772968-dotcom/ai-coding-guardrails/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-coding-guardrails))
- **ai-coding-handle**: Use when AutoClipboard installation or updates, AI coding handle Agent hooks, firmware version checks or updates, D4/V3 device identification, USB/serial/BLE connectivity, Linux btusb autosuspend or MediaTek controller firmware, or AutoClipboard and handle diagnostics need attention. ([Lijinzh/Communist-Manifesto-Releases](https://github.com/Lijinzh/Communist-Manifesto-Releases/tree/HEAD/plugins/zko-ai-coding-handle/skills/ai-coding-handle) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **ai-coding-implementation**: 面向 GitHub/Gitee 仓库的复杂业务代码实施流程。开发实现、复杂业务改造、多文件修改、测试修复、提交推送和创建 Pull Request 时使用。 ([Guo-Yixin/coding-agent](https://github.com/Guo-Yixin/coding-agent/tree/HEAD/agent/skills/ai-coding-implementation) · ★200)
- **ai-coding-interview**: Guide realistic AI-assisted coding interview rehearsals, timed mocks, and evidence-based scoring. Use for live backend AI coding interview practice that needs interviewer, coach, and coding-agent roles; do not use for ordinary feature implementation. ([saski/arnesto](https://github.com/saski/arnesto/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ai-coding-interview) · ★6)
- **ai-coding-interview**: Pair with a candidate during a live AI-enabled coding interview, where an interviewer watches the screen and asks about every line the candidate keeps. Use when the user says they are in an interview, a live coding round, or a screen-shared exercise (bug fix, feature, extend existing code). For the candidate's own prep checklist, read PREP.md. ([OrLeib/skills](https://github.com/OrLeib/skills/tree/HEAD/ai-coding-interview) · 2 copies)
- **ai-coding-interview**: Use when the user explicitly says this is an AI Coding interview, coding-agent interview, hand-written AI coding task, or wants to use Codex under a strict human-in-the-loop AI Coding interview workflow. This skill forces staged clarification, review gates, constraints, technical design, complexity classification, implementation staging, testing, and README summary instead of accepting a raw coding request directly. ([xtbnw/ai-coding-interview-skill](https://github.com/xtbnw/ai-coding-interview-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-coding-interview))
- **ai-coding-knowhow**: 大規模コードの改造・検証・レビューの規則集。計画を立てるとき、試験を書くとき、原因を調べるとき、レビューするときに読む ([yuucan2/ai-coding-starter-kit](https://github.com/yuucan2/ai-coding-starter-kit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ai-coding-knowhow))
- **ai-coding-learning-loop**: Run learning-aware AI coding work with explicit delegation modes, a confirmed Learning Contract, reviewed implementation Plan, verified implementation, a complete teaching Deliver, and Explain/Predict/Apply transfer gates. Use when a user wants AI to build or modify code while preserving understanding, asks for guided coding or full delegation with teaching, or needs separate engineering and learning evidence. ([SeRendizc/ai-coding-learning-loop](https://github.com/SeRendizc/ai-coding-learning-loop/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-coding-learning-loop))
- **ai-coding-memory**: 跨 coding agent 的个人/项目 memory 工具：从 Cursor / Claude Code / Aone Copilot / Qoder 沉淀对话 → 蒸馏成可召回的 markdown → 任意 IDE 跨界召回。 **此 Skill 处理两类场景**： 1) **首次安装后的 onboarding 验证**（30 秒走通全链路）—— TRIGGER：用户说 「我刚装了 ai-coding-memory」「测试 ai-memory」「ai-memory 怎么用」 「验证 ai-memory」「memory 工具能用吗」时 2) **批量消化 host_agent 模式的待蒸馏任务包**（last-7d/30d init 后产出的）—— TRIGGER：用户说 「整理今日记忆」「消化任务包」「跑一遍 memory pipeline」「沉淀今天的对话」 「distill 今日笔记」「批量整理记忆」「pending 任务」「整理 ai-memory」时 **不通过此 Skill 触发**： - 日常 coding 时的「自动召回历史经验」由 ai-coding-memory MCP Server 直接对接 IDE - 用户单条主动 `remember` 也通过 MCP 工具直接落盘，无需 Skill 编排 - 项目摘要 `project_context` 也是 MCP… ([Tigershan/ai-coding-memory](https://github.com/Tigershan/ai-coding-memory/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★1)
- **ai-coding-memory.collect**: Stage 1：采集 Aone Copilot / Cursor / Qoder 的 AI 对话记录到本地 JSON。 TRIGGER：用户说"采集今日对话"、"拉一下 IDE 对话"、"collect today"。 通常作为 ai-coding-memory pipeline 的第一步执行。 ([Tigershan/ai-coding-memory](https://github.com/Tigershan/ai-coding-memory/tree/HEAD/collect) · ★1)
- **ai-coding-memory.distill**: Stage 2：把 collect 阶段的对话流清洗为「自描述、可复用」的 topic 块。 采用 Agent 编排模式：脚本只生成提示词与状态机，由宿主 Agent（你）逐步消化， 把 LLM 输出回写到任务包文件。 TRIGGER：用户说「清洗今日对话」「distill today」「提炼笔记」「跑一下 distill」 「继续昨天的 distill」「整理今天的 AI 编码记忆」时。 ([Tigershan/ai-coding-memory](https://github.com/Tigershan/ai-coding-memory/tree/HEAD/distill) · ★1)
- **ai-coding-memory.mcp-server**: Stage 4：MCP Server，让 IDE 在编码时按需召回个人编码知识库。 本模块**不通过 SKILL TRIGGER 触发**，而是作为常驻 MCP Server 被 IDE 自动拉起， 通过 stdio 协议暴露三个工具给 IDE 模型按需调用。 TRIGGER（针对模型本身）：当用户提及「以前」「上次」「之前怎么处理」「我记得」 等回顾性表述，或问题涉及他特定项目/历史经验时，模型应主动调用 search_memory。 ([Tigershan/ai-coding-memory](https://github.com/Tigershan/ai-coding-memory/tree/HEAD/mcp-server) · ★1)
